# Tries ledger: every change tried for an entry, per test board

Started 2026-11-03 by the AI assistant under the captain's delegation of 2026-11-02, after the captain asked for
continuous iteration with no fixed timeline and a side agent warned that endless tweaking against the same few
backtests lets lucky fits through even when the adoption rule passes. The rule's 10 seeds rule out draw noise, not a
change that only suits the particular released stages a backtest uses.

From 2026-11-04 the ledger also carries the captain's guiding principle (quoted in `AGENTS.md`): rules 5-8 below and
the headroom tables of where the entries stand against the published baselines, written by the AI assistant under the
same delegation. Rows written before that date stay as they were.

## Rules

1. Every change tried for an entry gets a row here **before its run**: the biological or physical reason, the metrics
   where its gain should show, and what would make a gain a fit to one backtest rather than a real effect. The row is
   committed before the run starts; the row names that commit.
2. A change is kept only if all three hold:
   - the adoption rule passes (`vec.adoption`, `docs/PLAN.md` section 3, item 8);
   - the gain sits on the metrics its reason predicts;
   - no known feature of one backtest explains it. Example: the E7.25 embryo target sits much closer to E6.75 than
     its day says (2026-11-03 diagnostic below), so a change that moves the mixture toward the earlier bracket gains
     there and says nothing about E7.75, where the bracket fraction is 1/2. A second (added 2026-11-04 from the
     rebuild design, wf_237f8d8f-e7c): by mmd_u both embryo backtests prefer equal mixing to their nominal
     fraction (0.0152 against 0.0264 on E7.25, 0.0380 against 0.0433 on E7.5; the 2026-11-03 table below), so a
     change that pulls either backtest's mixture toward 1/2 gains on both and says nothing at test, where the
     nominal fraction is already 1/2.
3. Changes without a reason that holds at test are pruned before they run, with the reason written down.
4. Every try counts, diagnostics included; each value of a swept parameter is one try. The counts go into every
   adoption report.
5. **The baseline and the question.** Every row, written before its run, names its board's published baseline
   (copy_last on T1, the heart and the embryo; wt_identity on T3) and the entry or candidate it changes. It answers the
   captain's question: what meaningful change beyond the baseline does it predict, and what held-out evidence would
   show it? The change names its facet: which states emerge, their proportions, their expression or, on T2, their
   spatial organisation. The evidence is named in advance: the backtests or folds that count for it under the
   adoption rule, none it was tuned or selected on (on T3 the cross-knockout folds; the Gata4 replicate fold never
   counts). The row also states the gain it expects there; a change expected to gain less than the adoption rule's
   0.3 points on its counting backtests is pruned before it runs (rule 3), since it could pass only by luck. Its
   outcome reports the result against the floor as well as against the entry. No test-board score is evidence.
6. **Weakest open group first.** Every row names the scored group it targets by its `vec.boards.GROUPS` id, its weight
   and its line in the headroom tables ("Where the entries stand against the published baselines"). A board's next
   try targets its weakest group (the most points left on the counting backtests; `docs/PLAN.md` section 3, item 9)
   whose lever is open; a try on another group says why the weaker ones are closed or have no reason that holds at
   test.
   - **Ties.** When a board's counting backtests name different weakest groups, those groups count as tied; across
     boards, gaps within the spread of a board's stand-ins count as tied. The row says which it targets first and why.
   - **Closed levers.** A lever is closed by the post-exposure block (`docs/DATA_POLICY.md`, 2026-11-04, (b) to (d):
     cell numbers, sizes and scope on every board; T3's composition; T1's composition except by the legal route of
     (d)), by a ruling of the captain (the heart's composition trend, dropped on 2026-11-01, `docs/PLAN.md`
     4.3, pipeline step 2), by a standing working answer (no new cross-modal input enters an upload until the
     organisers answer, A15) or by this ledger after its tries (the depth, blend, herding and coupling lines; the
     captain can reopen one). A lever fixed by a frozen or pre-registered rule moves only as that rule allows or by the
     captain's own ruling: on T3 a factor change that passes the adoption rule on the cross-knockout folds goes to him
     (`docs/PLAN.md` decision log 2026-11-01 (d)), and `experiments/t3_decomp/rules.yaml` is not edited. A closed
     lever stays closed whatever its headroom.
7. **Developmental reasons.** A developmental reason makes a change testable: it names the metrics the change should
   move and on which backtests. It never keeps a step the adoption rule rejects, and a gain off the predicted metrics
   is not kept (rule 2). An extra part of a change (a parameter, a step, a data source) needs its own held-out gain,
   measured with and without it, each run counted (rule 4); without that gain it is dropped.
8. **The tables follow the entries.** After every kept change or new entry, the script is rerun from committed
   results only (`python3 -I experiments/harness/headroom.py`), its output in `results/headroom/` is committed, and
   the headroom tables are copied from `headroom.txt`, naming the commit they were built at. An entry the script does
   not cover yet is added to its board list first. No official test score enters the tables.

## Where the entries stand against the published baselines

Built on 2026-11-04 by `experiments/harness/headroom.py` from the committed results at fcd8964 (stdlib only:
`python3 -I experiments/harness/headroom.py`; output in `results/headroom/headroom.txt` and `headroom.json`, with the
sha256 of its 19 inputs; the script and its output are added by the commit that adds this section). It reads
committed practice-task results only, never a target, a test-board file or a cell count, and it runs no candidate, so
it is not a try. Its self-checks: every stored board score recomputed from its skills to 1.4e-14 points, and every
mean used within 0.0005 of `results/registry.csv`.

Each cell gives a scored group's points against the published baseline, 100 x weight x (skill - 0.5), seed-paired,
then its points left to the split-half ceiling, 100 x weight x (1 - skill), on one backtest the adoption rule counts
for the entry (10 seeds). The baseline scores 50 on every seed of every backtest. * marks a stand-in, used where no
backtest can run the entry. "copy" is a fresh draw of the reference stage's real cells at the count rule, the
baseline as we would submit it: it is not the scorer's own working copy, so it can score below 50. The columns are
the `vec.boards.GROUPS` ids, which `headroom.txt` prints: T1 de_recovery, direction, distribution, covariance; T2
expression_change, cell_state, shape_growth, local_organisation (neighbourhoods); T3 response_identity,
response_direction, response_magnitude, mutant_state.

**Across boards**, in Overall units (s1 or its stand-ins; `headroom.txt`, "Points left per scored group in Overall
units"): the largest gaps are T3 response identity 15.18 (11.89 with stand-in A), T1 distribution 15.08, T1 DE
recovery 13.00, T1 covariance 11.45, T3 direction 11.29 (8.05), T3 mutant state 10.08 (5.70) and T1 direction 9.49;
then the heart's four groups (expression change 6.27, cell state 6.22, shape 6.22, neighbourhoods 5.82) and embryo
cell state 5.75. The first two are tied (rule 6): T3's lies within its stand-ins' spread (11.89 to 15.18), and both
are a 0.30-weight metric at the floor. Points left in all ("Points left in Overall units per board"): T1 49.03, T3
39.23 (26.67 with stand-in A), heart 24.53, embryo 20.02.

Read with care:
- Points left are measured to the split-half ceiling, which no prediction reaches, and the skill map is 1/(1 + x), so
  equal points left are not equal reach. Near the floor (the M1 and H1 stand-ins, T3's s2) the ranking mostly
  restates the weights.
- These are backtests, not the test: T1 on E9.5 -> E10.5 (test E10.5 -> E12.5), whose stand-in draws E9.5's chamber
  mix against E10.5's chamber-cut truth; the heart 1 and 1.75 days ahead (test 2); the embryo at t = 1/3 and 2/3
  (test 1/2). Whether a backtest's weakest group is the test's is assumption A22.
- Earlier tries already aimed at weak groups (a check, not a change to their rows): T5 at B0's DE recovery on T1
  (de_score skill 0.450 on the chamber cut), H3 at the heart's cell state (-0.32 on the 1-day backtest), E7 and E8 at
  fgw's cell state (weak on E7.25 only), E11 at fgw's shape on E7.5 (its one group below the baseline there).

### What it means for the order of the next tries

1. **T1 composition by the legal route.** Births by the legal route were designed and pruned on 2026-11-04
   (wf_77e506c8-014; "Pruned before running"). `docs/DATA_POLICY.md` 2026-11-04 (d) then reopened T1's shares within the
   chamber scope by the legal route, and (f) says how a share rule is built (quotas at the frozen count, never
   importance weights; T1 depth closed). The rebuild design of 2026-11-04 (wf_237f8d8f-e7c) registered no composition
   try: no legal share rule has a counted check, since both counted T1 tasks draw E9.5's chamber mix (cardiomyocytes
   about 64% of cells, against about 19% in the chamber-cut truth: the pruned exact top-up below), and the committed
   oracle-base grid (`experiments/s2_hedge/`) puts the current s1/s2 pair within about 1.5 points of the best pair under
   every prior it states. A share change can enter only by the captain's ruling after he writes a prior (item 6). What
   remained on T1 was R1, a ruling request: cells without a Feng group keep their E10.5 expression (row below), kept by
   the captain's ruling of 2026-11-05. A later composition row still follows rules 5-8 and names that confound in
   advance.
2. **T3 response identity.** The largest gap in Overall units on the cross-knockout stand-in (15.18); on stand-in A,
   s1's own factor, it is 11.89, behind T1 distribution (15.08) and T1 DE recovery (13.00). So it ties with T1
   distribution (rule 6), and T1 went first; the rebuild design found no T1 try (item 1) and no T3 candidate with a
   reason that holds at test ("Pruned before running"). A factor change must pass the adoption rule on the two
   cross-knockout folds and then goes to the captain; the composition is closed. The one reading the principle asks
   for, s1's own lead component (the Gata4 factor, without composition) on the Gata4 -> Mab21l2 fold, the first
   reading of it on a knockout it was not fitted on (A10), is registered as K2a with a permuted-factor control K2b
   (rows below; decided by the AI assistant under the delegation of 2026-11-02, the captain can overrule). It changes
   no upload: T3's s1 changes only by the captain's ruling, whatever the outcome.
3. **Embryo: stop polishing.** It has the smallest gap (20.02 Overall points left) and the most tries (9), no group
   has an open lever with a reason that holds at test, and its one measured test risk, the flattened s1 geometry, is
   hedged by s2.
4. **Heart: no try.** Its entry is copy_last at the count rule plus a small count-space momentum, within 1.3 points of
   the baseline on every metric, and the pre-registered size, which no backtest can score. No lever on it has a
   reason that holds at test (above).
5. **T1 direction: no other try.** Feng's E9.5 lies inside every T1 window, and both T1 backtests that count for a
   same-scope change (the whole and the chamber-cut E10.5 truths) carry E9.5's chamber mix, so no check exists for a
   change to Feng's direction.
6. **After the first wave.** No further try is registered without a new reason that holds at test, written in its row
   before the run. The rebuild design's second-wave items run only at the captain's request: H5, a placebo-controlled
   reading of B0's momentum on both heart backtests (information only); D3, pricing T1's cardiomyocyte tail, which
   changes a frozen s2 rule and runs only after the captain writes a prior (under the stated priors it loses, 68.28
   against 67.89 with a uniform prior; break-even needs about 25% of the prior on a cardiomyocyte share of at least 0.8;
   not run: no prior was written by 2026-11-05, and it lowers the chance of a high total,
   `experiments/gate/options_tail.py`); T3's s2 moved to the barred branch's s1 (a frozen rule; it would override D11b's
   failed bar after the fact; not recommended; re-examined on 2026-11-05 and rejected after an adversarial review,
   `docs/PLAN.md` decision log 2026-11-05 (d)); a zone-fixed Feng cardiomyocyte direction (about 0 +- 0.3; not
   recommended). Scenarios kept as risks and not built (closed levers): T1 depth and the oesophagus-junction cells; the
   heart's core-focused s2 (scope; a frozen rule); stack-limited embryo geometry (z extent; a frozen rule); T3's
   composition magnitude (a frozen rule).

The captain's upload gate (2026-11-05; `docs/PLAN.md` section 8): no first upload on any board until our estimated
leaderboard total is above 209 (about 184 today) or his deadline exception opens (the s1 methods freeze at 2026-11-27
00:00 UTC; from then the first round may go in, each upload only on his go-ahead at the time), so every entry stays
open to tries until then. The gate is about uploads only: it is never evidence for a try, and a try's row still names
its own expected gain on its counting backtests.

Once the heart and embryo s1 are uploaded, the frozen s2 rules build their s2 as s1, at s1's commit
(`docs/S2_RULES.md`), so from then on no try reaches those two boards unless the captain changes a rule.

## Tries since 2026-11-03

Order of decisions, fixed before the run: E7 against fgw (the s1 entry); then E8 against whichever of fgw and E7 is
the entry after that decision. Both on the two embryo backtests, 10 seeds, by `experiments/harness/run.py decide`.

| # | Board | Candidate | Reason | Gain should show on | A fit, not an effect, if | Registered at | Outcome |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| E7 | embryo | `fgw_depth` | Detection depth differs several-fold between the released embryo stages (median counts per cell E6.75 30, E7.25 35, E7.5 62, E8.0 212; `results/diag_emb_mix/`), and the scorer reads log(1 + 1e4 c / N), whose zeros and levels follow depth. fgw's mixture carries both brackets' depths (at test half its cells at 62 and half at 212). The target's depth is predicted log-linearly between the brackets' medians at the bracket fraction (`vec.t2_build.DEPTH_RULE`; at test about 115), and cells of a deeper bracket are binomially thinned to it, the counts a shallower acquisition of the same cell would give. Positions and draws are fgw's | variogram and mmd_u above all, then neighborhood_mmd; de_score and de_direction may move either way (depth moves pseudobulk); the shape terms identical to fgw's | it gains on one task only; or the predicted depth happens to sit near one target's depth while the rule misses the other (log-linear gives 64 against E7.5's 62 and 49 against E7.25's 35), so the gain should hold on both | f68836d | **Not kept** (`results/emb_depth_v1/`, 10 seeds): E7.5 -4.18, ahead in 0 of 10 (veto: mmd_u 0.671 -> 0.598, variogram 0.664 -> 0.602, neighborhood_mmd 0.640 -> 0.557); E7.25 +0.74, ahead in 10 of 10. Why: a mixture drawn (1 - t) : t already has the target's average log depth (35^(2/3) x 212^(1/3) = 64 against E7.5's 62), so thinning the deeper bracket pulled the population's average below the target; on E7.25 the mixture's average (49) sat above the truth (35), and thinning moved it toward it. What the scorer rewards is the population's average depth, which the mixture already gets right at the nominal fraction |
| E8 | embryo | `fgw_blend` | Development is continuous: an E7.75 cell is an intermediate state between its E7.5 ancestor's and its E8.0 descendant's, which a mixture of whole cells of the two brackets does not contain. Each drawn cell's counts become a binomial blend of itself and a partner from the other bracket chosen through fgw's coupling, splitting the expected counts (1 - t) : t at E7's predicted depth (a shallow side that cannot supply its share is capped at all its counts) | mmd_u, variogram and neighborhood_mmd beyond E7's; de_score and de_direction about unchanged; the shape terms identical to fgw's | it gains on one task only; or the gain comes from the blend's narrower within-state spread suiting one target (a blend averages two cells, so it carries about half a real cell's cell-to-cell variation) | f68836d | **Not kept**: E7.5 -2.23 and E7.25 -3.19, ahead in 0 of 10 on each (mmd_u 0.671 -> 0.638 and 0.497 -> 0.429; neighborhood_mmd falls on both). Blended cells are too narrow a population: the targets' cells are discrete real states, which a mixture of real cells matches better than averages of two |

| H3 | heart | `b0_depth` | (Restated after E7 and E8 and before this try's run: what the scorer rewards is the population's average log depth; E7 failed because it moved that average away from the target, and gained where it moved it toward the truth.) Counts per cell fall steadily through heart development in the released series (median counts per cell: E8.25_late 233, E8.5 166, E8.75 130, E9.5 75, E10.5 56; `results/diag_depth/`), and the scorer reads log(1 + 1e4 c / N), whose zeros and levels follow depth. B0 keeps E10.5's depth. The target's depth is extrapolated at B0's own damping: half the latest log rate per day, carried over the horizon (`vec.t2_build.DEPTH_EXTRAP_RULE`; at test 56 x (56/75)^(0.5 x 2) = 42); B0's cells are binomially thinned to it, never deepened. Leave-one-out on the depth series alone, each stage from the two before it: E8.75 134 (observed 130), E9.5 90 (75), E10.5 52 (56) | variogram, mmd_u, neighborhood_mmd; de_score and de_direction may move either way; the shape terms identical to B0's | it gains on one task only. Caveat written before the run: the survey that found the trend also showed both backtests' target depth (E10.5's 56), so the form of the rule (per-day rate, B0's damping 0.5, the size procedure's convention) was written knowing it, and E7's outcome was seen before this row was restated; the damping is B0's existing value, not fitted, and the leave-one-out above is the check that does not use a scored target | 71b18f9 | **Not kept** (`results/heart_depth_v1/`): the 1-day backtest -3.92, ahead in 0 of 10 (veto: mmd_u 0.466 -> 0.401, neighborhood_mmd 0.517 -> 0.458), although the extrapolated depth (52) was close to the truth's (56); the 1.75-day backtest +0.45, ahead in 9 of 10 (de_score and variogram up, mmd_u down). Across E7 and H3, thinning lowered mmd_u and neighborhood_mmd in three of four cases, whether or not it matched the target's depth: binomially thinned cells are not like a shallower acquisition's cells to these metrics. The depth line is closed (3 tries, none kept) |
| T5 | T1 | `b0_herd` | Sampling noise in a prediction's pseudobulk costs de_score: the metric ranks each gene's change of the mean log expression, the floor's change is exactly zero, and a fresh random draw of the reference's own cells scores -0.52 raw (skill 0.38) on E9.5 -> E10.5 (`results/t1_de_v1/`). Herding is a variance reduction with no biological content: within each category's quota, a drawn cell is swapped for an undrawn cell of the same category when that brings the sample's per-gene mean (log1p CP10k, all genes) closer to the category pool's mean (`vec.core.herd_rows`, random proposals, 20 per drawn cell, its own random stream so the draws it starts from are B0's); the same holds at test, since the scorer is the same | de_score above all, then de_direction; mmd_u and variogram about unchanged or slightly better (a more representative sample) | it gains on one task only; or it lowers mmd_u or variogram (herding toward the mean could favour typical cells and thin the tails) | 2bf32c4 | **Not kept** (`results/t1_herd_v1/`): -0.03 on E9.5 -> E10.5 and -0.01 on the chamber cut, ahead in 4 and 5 of 10 seeds. Why: most of the noise in a prediction's change comes from the scorer's own reference working copy (10% of the reference stage: 1,706 cells of E9.5 against B0's 5,118), which herding our draw cannot remove |
| T6 | T1 | `copy_herd` (diagnostic) | The same herding on a plain copy of the reference: measures how much of the copy's de_score handicap is sampling noise. Reported, not a candidate for an entry | de_score | - | 2bf32c4 | Small: +0.06, +0.21 and +0.17 on the three T1 backtests. The copy's handicap is the reference's noise, not ours |

| E10 | embryo | `fgw_aligned` | fgw's coupling is an exact one-to-one pairing of its 2,000 anchors per stage: with Euclidean within-cloud distances the square-loss Gromov-Wasserstein term is concave on the couplings, so POT's conditional gradient ends on a vertex. Neither its features (panel expression at 30-212 counts per cell) nor the within-cloud distances can tell an embryo's left side from its right where expression is left-right symmetric (no lateralised marker before about E8.0), so partners can sit at random or mirrored across that direction, and McCann moves then pull both brackets towards its mid-plane: the moved cloud flattens there (wholly at t = 1/2 with mirrored partners), and so does the s2's mapped E8.0 half. The proper rotation is mostly unaffected (the other two axes and det +1 fix it). Change: keep FGW's rotation; take the moves (still the mean displacement of the 8 nearest anchors) from an exact transport of the same anchors on (1 - a) M / max M + a \|x - y R\|^2 / max, in the before stage's frame, a = INTERP_ALPHA = 0.5 reused (`vec.core.aligned_coupling`; `interpolate_clouds(..., move_coupling="aligned")`); in a common frame position tells the side. Synthetic left-right symmetric pair (tests/test_t2_build.py, AI-assistant scratch): FGW's partners have whitened cross-correlation about 0 along the symmetric axis, the aligned ones 0.4-0.7 (300-500 anchors), and the moved cloud's thinnest SD returns to the brackets'. **Gate, target-free, fixed here before it runs** (decided by the AI assistant under the captain's delegation of 2026-11-02, as the ideas workflow proposed): `experiments/harness/diag_emb_coupling.py` on the three bracket pairs at build seeds 1,000,000-1,000,009. A draw has the defect if fgw's smallest whitened anchor-partner cross-correlation is below 0.3 or its moved cloud's thinnest SD over RMS radius is below 0.9 x the thinner bracket's; the fix works if the aligned partners' cross-correlation is at least 0.6 and their merged-label agreement at most 0.05 below fgw's. The try runs only if draws with both reach 6 of 10 on each backtest pair and 3 of 10 (or the s1 seed's draw) on the test pair. A defect at test that the backtests cannot show: pruned, reported to the captain as a test-only risk. The diagnostic reads no target and no score, so it is not a try | E7.5: d2_shape up in at least 8 of 10 seeds, occupancy_dice flat or up; E7.25: d2 and Dice up in the seeds whose draws show the defect, about unchanged in the rest; de_score, de_direction, mmd_u and variogram identical to fgw's (the same cells); scale_log_ratio identical up to the scorer's rounding; neighborhood_mmd within +-0.01 skill on both | the gain sits in Dice alone, or only in E7.25's seeds 3, 4, 5, 8 and 9 (that block's anisotropy: E7.25's truth is thinner along PC3 than either bracket); or mean_anchor_shift falls well below fgw's while d2 moves toward fgw_shape0's (shorter moves across the sectioning-plane change, not the mechanism); or the gain is confined to draws where the rotation is a half-turn off (rotation and label agreement reported per seed). Caveats written before the run: the idea came after E7.25's seed split was seen; a = 0.5 is reused in a new role and no other value is tried; the backtests sit at t = 1/3 and 2/3, so a pass says nothing about t = 1/2 beyond the diagnostic | 5baba46 | **Pruned by its gate, not run, not counted** (`results/diag_emb_coupling/`, at 5baba46, clean). The defect showed in 10 of 10 draws on the test pair and on E7.5, and in 5 of 10 on E7.25 (seeds 3, 4, 5, 8 and 9, the seeds of fgw's earlier bimodal split there: mirrored partners), short of the 6 the gate needs on each backtest pair; the fix worked in every draw of all three pairs. Re-registered as E11 (below) |

Each bracket keeps its nominal count exactly and each stratum its share of the bracket (vec.core.quotas); draws are weighted without replacement, with repeats only if a stratum's quota exceeds its pool; random streams 8 and 9. Composition, sizes, scope and depth are closed; this is an expression change with a reason that holds at test (the filter uses only the reference stage's own cells, whatever the stage) | B0 moves each harmonised lineage by half its last per-gene log-ratio change, a vector dominated at these read depths by per-gene offsets (run effects, a depth-linked detection trend) and sampling noise. Real maturation programmes are coordinated across genes and appear as covariation among cells of a lineage within one stage, so the momentum is projected onto those directions. The rule, fixed here (`t2_build.CFM_RULE`): for a lineage with at least 200 reference-stage cells with counts, the log1p CP10k of those cells is residualised, gene by gene, on a polynomial of degree 5 in the standardised log panel counts per cell (the depth axis, with the intercept; this removes it, as the review required), divided per gene by the residual SD and clipped at +-5 (no rescaling after). K_lineage is the number of eigenvalues of that covariance above the Marchenko-Pastur edge (1 + sqrt(p / n))^2, p the genes that vary among those cells and are not held flat, n the residual degrees of freedom (cells minus the fitted terms); nothing is tuned per lineage. The momentum divided by the same per-gene SD is projected onto those eigenvectors and multiplied back by the SD. Norm rule: the filtered factor is renormalised to the lineage's unfiltered L2 norm over the genes the filter acts on, so the candidate is not a partial hold. K = 0, or fewer than 200 reference cells: the lineage keeps B0's factor; a gene that does not vary among those cells keeps its factor. Held-flat handling: as B0, Untracked cells (a stage-tagged type with no cross-stage momentum) and lineages with fewer than 20 cells at a stage are never filtered; B0 on T2 passes no held-flat genes, and a held gene would be left out of the filter and the norm and stay at f = 1. The settings (degree 5, clip +-5, 200 cells as designed) were read from synthetic counts only (no project data, no stage), and only this one configuration is run | Expression only (facet: expression; no composition, count, size or scope change; same cells, expected depth kept). Rule, fixed now: q-hat = (mean B-perp - 49.1371) / (60.7745 - 49.1371) over the 20 rows. q-hat is a share of score on Gata4, not N11's probability q: B keeps its stage term, so if stage does not carry, B scores below what B-perp scored; it is fed to N11 as a stated stand-in. (i) q-hat >= 0.75 (B-perp >= B - 2.9) and severity floored in at most 2 of 20 rows: report q >= 0.75 for N11's grid and recommend the swap for the no-answer and zero-answer branches only, provided the N11 rerun with U3d's measured cells at q = q-hat (clipped to 0-1) meets N10's bar at both N11 bands (0.7, 0.65) and (0.7, 0.5): mixture dE_T3 >= +0.5 and dP_top3 >= 0 at the 90th and 95th levels for lambda 0.5 and 1. Replicates of one file agree only at Spearman 0.72 (D11a), so the part of a gene's ratio that is not reproducible across its own file's sections enters the factor as signal, including position-driven genes whose ratio depends on which sections were cut. The knockout file's own sections measure that instability, and an empirical-Bayes posterior mean (prior mean 0 on the log ratio, the no-change prior) shrinks unstable genes towards f = 1 more than stable ones, which suits ranking metrics (de_score, a top-list overlap; de_direction, a rank partial correlation). It adds no data: only the files a fold already holds, the noise taken from source files, never from a fold's target. On the counting folds a pass needs a real gain of about +0.5 or more on each fold on K2-like seed noise (the design's scratch power arithmetic: about 0.2% for a null change, 5% for a true +0.3 on each fold, 18% for +0.5, 54% for +0.8). The expected gain is below rule 5's 0.3-point prune; the campaign of 2026-11-09 (a) lifts that prune, since the run buys evidence on a lever never measured. Procedure, fixed here: per chamber category with cells at both stages (fewer than 20 cells at a stage or stand-ins at a stage get no factors, as B0's momentum; at the backtests' E8.5 start the config's remap of Endothelium makes endocardium its own, vascular and epicardium are stand-ins and get none, so the backtest is mostly a CM reading), Sinkhorn from start to reference in a PCA of the fold's inputs (the start and reference stages pooled; T9's HVG, PCA, cost, epsilon and stopping constants), k-means metacells of the reference cells (T9's K rule), per metacell the log ratio of its own mean CP10k over its transported start-stage partners' coupling-weighted mean CP10k (pseudocount 0.5), the per-category cell-weighted mean of the log factor removed per gene, damped by B0's 0.5, then values below log 1.05 in absolute value set to 0 and the rest clipped to +-log 3 (on the damped factor). Fitted once per loaded fold. At test the same rule would run with start E9.5 and reference E10.5, R1's held types excluded (promotion needs the captain's ruling, rule 2) | Facet: within-type expression states on B0's cells. Metrics: mmd_u and the variogram up on the counted cuts, de_score and de_direction flat within noise since the edit is mean-free in log space. Expected gain about +0.2 to +0.8 board points, P(keep) about 15-20% by judgement, no run yet; the campaign (decision log 2026-11-09 (a)) lifts rule 5's prune, not the adoption rule | `decide(b0, g_mom)` on the two counted cuts T1/E9.5->E10.5 and T1/E9.5->E10.5:chamber (configs/pseudo_tasks.yaml; protocol bake, build main, seeds 0-9, same-scope candidates so both cuts count; T1/E8.5->E9.5 is not counted because the candidate needs a start stage). Kept only if the adoption rule keeps it on both cuts (paired mean gain above 0.3 points and ahead in at least 8 of 10 seeds on each, no cut losing more than 0.3 points) and rule 2 holds on both: at least two thirds of the mean gain on each cut sits on the distribution and covariance groups (`experiments/t1_scope/o1_readout.py gmom`). A kept result goes to the captain; it changes no upload by itself. Not kept: the G line stops before any X002/X005 ruling is asked, whatever T9's bar says (the line needs both T9's bar cleared and T11 kept); T8-T10 are descriptive of the ceiling and do not rescue it | The E9.5 -> E10.5 momentum spans the dissection change from an E9.5 embryo portion to a dissected E10.5 heart, as B0's did, so a gain carried by one cut only, or by a composition feature of that step, is not the within-type effect; a gain sitting on de_score or de_direction rather than mmd_u and the variogram fails rule 2 above; and because E8.5 has own CM but only stand-ins for vascular and epicardium, a pass is mostly a CM result | the wave-1 registration commit, before its run | **Run at 203c623, counted** (T1 try 11). **Adoption rule passes**: `g_mom` against `b0` +1.13 on T1/E9.5->E10.5 and +1.23 on :chamber, ahead 10 of 10 on each, no veto; share of the gain on distribution and covariance 0.76 and 0.74 (rule: at least 0.67) (`experiments/harness/results/t11_g_mom/`, `experiments/t1_scope/results/t11_g_mom_groups/`). Rule, every constant fixed here (`vec.t1_scope` DP_*; a change is a new row). (a) Selection, the same in the test build and the backtests, nothing fitted on any transition's truth (the predicted-change table of the first draft is dropped: its change axis was scope-confounded on E9.5 -> E10.5): level bin = rank of the FULL reference's mean log1p CP10k, highest first, edges 100, 200, 400, 800, 1600, 3200; change bin = rank of dp = pb(prediction) - pb(full reference), most negative first: 0-399 the tail, 400-1599 near it, the middle, top 1600 predicted up. Eligible: level rank below 800 (the first four level bins; the thinning's first-order solve meets the band there at T1's depth), change bin near-tail or flat (never the tail, never predicted up), pb(reference) >= 0.25, not held (R2's union and the pre-registered list). Order: the level bin alone, ties by the lowest dp, then name; the first 200 whose target is reachable are promoted (200 is a constant, half the strong-null down list; no test count enters it). (b) Realisation (`apply_down_prior`, after M1's steps, `apply_factors` keep_depth=True, stream [seed, 4]): only the cells M1 moves along Feng's direction; each promoted gene's dp goes to the band -0.15 by one per-gene factor f >= 0.5 in the covered cells (genes needing less are skipped), solved from the exact first-order slope of binomial thinning with three fixed-point steps for keep_depth's constant; other genes move only by that constant. (d) Target-free checks in the build, the file written only if all hold: exactly 200 genes promoted; the scorer's mmd_unbiased(file after, file before) on the panel at most 0.0035 raw (at most 5000 cells, the same before and after; the covered cells' mean shift in 30 PCs of the pre-prior file reported, deciding nothing); at least 95% of promoted genes realise dp <= band + 0.02; expected library of every covered cell unchanged (exact), realised mean library ratio within 1%; uncovered cells and held genes exactly unchanged; other panel genes' mean log1p moves within -0.01 and 1.5 x the largest log depth constant + 0.01. If a check refuses, no strong-null file is written, T1's files stay M1 with R1 and R2 as built, the numbers go to the captain and no constant is relaxed (a change is a new row). Expected gain: if the regime transfers about +1.2 to +1.75 T1 on s1 (200 genes at P about 0.5 displacing tail genes at about 0.2, the approved +1.75), less on s2: band -0.15 sits near rank 153 of s1's committed dp tail and 273 of s2's (`experiments/research_2026_11/results/t1-tail.json`, `tails.json`), so the promoted block holds about ranks 153-353 in s1, all inside the bottom 382-426, and 273-473 in s2, about 55-77% of it inside s2's bottom n_dn slots at the strong-null transition's n_dn (somewhat more after the depth constant). If the regime does not transfer about -0.2 to -0.8 T1 by the scorer's formulas (mmd_u about -0.1 to -0.6 at d_f 0.08-0.11, more at a smaller d_f, the test's being hidden; variogram about -0.02; de_direction about 0 to -0.1; de_score only if n_dn exceeds about 150), against the approved -0.33; part (c) measures it | None counts for the test regime: no backtest can show the bet's payoff, and the regime at E12.5 is the unknown. (c) The downside is measured before any build, by the harness's paired scoring (10 seeds, bake protocol, scorer build main, build seeds 1,000,000 + s), counted as tries T12a and T12b (rule 4, K2a and K2b's precedent), so T1 goes up by 2, from 11 to 13: T12a, `b0_strong_null` against `b0` on T1/E9.5->E10.5 and its chamber cut (a strong-null truth, the regime the bet is built for); T12b, `scope_strong_null` against `b0_scope` on T1/E8.5->E9.5 (its own row). Stop rule fixed now (`stop_rule.py`), means of the 10 paired differences: S1, on any of the three tasks the total board points or the distribution and covariance groups together (mmd_u 0.30, variogram 0.20) below -0.66 (twice the approved -0.33; break-even moves from about 16% to about 27%, the top of sheet C's 0.2-0.3); S2, on the control T1/E8.5->E9.5, raw mmd_u rise above 0.0035, raw variogram rise above 0.0004, or the two groups together below -0.33 points. Points are not comparable across tasks because the skill map's slope is the operating point's: one raw unit of mmd_u costs -52.4 points at b0_scope's operating point on the control, -61.0 and -68.6 at b0's on the E9.5 -> E10.5 tasks, -68 to -94 near the floor at d_f 0.11 to 0.08 and -312 near the floor at the control's d_f 0.024, hence raw units on the control. 0.0035 raw mmd_u is 0.33 points near the floor at d_f 0.08 (-1.09 at d_f 0.024, -0.53 at 0.05, -0.24 at 0.11; -0.18 at the control's own operating point); 0.0004 raw variogram is 0.36 points near the floor at d_f 0.0055 (-2.22 at 0.0009, -0.67 at 0.003, -0.20 at 0.01; -0.34 at the control's operating point). The test's d_f is hidden, so `stop_rule.py` reports the implied points for those d_f on every task. Verdicts: STOP if S1, S2, an incomplete run or a non-finite mean holds (the build stops, the numbers go to the captain); captain if no stop holds but any task's total or distribution-plus-covariance points lie below -0.33 (the measured cost is not near the price he approved: the numbers go to him first and the build proceeds only on his explicit reading, 2026-11-10 (d)); proceed otherwise, and even then the change enters only by the captain's ruling (the adoption rule is not its test), the build only after `approved: true` and its target-free checks. Read H = T1/E8.5->E9.5:cm (E8.5's cardiomyocytes to E9.5's, within type), the read no counted candidate has run on. Named known feature, not a clean read: the direction readout's smoke read Feng's real direction within cardiomyocytes on exactly this transition with the scorer's metric family (the committed experiments/t1_direction/results/smoke_E8.5_E9.5/per_type.csv, type CM, predictor feng_E14.5, target full, held flat: sign agreement 0.717, Spearman 0.428, de_direction 0.296, de_score 0.084 against a chance of 0.056; predictor feng_E15.5: 0.681, 0.374, 0.265, 0.067). The within-type E10.5 reads T1/E9.5->E10.5:cm and T1/E9.5->E10.5:endocardium (target, reference and inputs cut to one chamber category, so a CM-enriched gene class is not judged through E9.5's chamber mix; E10.5's earlier reads by the pre-registered readout and M1's diagnostic diag_v1 are a named known feature). Reported and deciding nothing: the three whole-population T1 tasks of configs/pseudo_tasks.yaml and the comparison with B0 and the floor. Before the gate runs: N20's stamped yes at this commit, and `late_proxy.py preflight`, the floor and anchors of the three within-type reads on all ten seeds with no candidate built; an unscorable within-type read stops the tournament there, no try run or counted. After the preflight, a within-type read that cannot be scored fails the whole gate, and the tries count. Gate (T13-T15, stage gate of late_proxy.py): lp_proxy passes the adoption rule's per-task test against b0_scope (paired mean gain above 0.3, ahead in at least 8 of 10 seeds) on H and on at least one within-type E10.5 read; on each read it passes, it also beats Cflip and Cperm by that test and its margin sits on the rank metrics (the de_recovery and direction margins together above 0 and at least half of the total margin, a coded condition of the pass: a fit stops the variants); neither control itself passes it on H; lp_proxy loses more than 0.3 on average on none of the three within-type reads. Variants (T16-T18, stage variants, run only if the gate passes, T18 also only if N20's stop lets it; a variant not run does not count): T16 and T18 against lp_proxy and T17 against T16 each pass only with mean gain above 0.3 and at least 9 of 10 seeds ahead on H and on both within-type E10.5 reads, the margin on the rank metrics on each, and no within-type mean loss above 0.3. Seeds check draw noise only (the ledger's opening), so the seed bar is no multiplicity correction; multiplicity is carried by the try count, stated in every report, and by asking for the gain on all three within-type reads. Landing, fixed now: no outcome changes an upload by itself, and since no counted task can run M1's real direction (E9.5 -> E14.5), a change to it can land only by the captain's ruling. Gate fails: M1's direction stays as ruled, nothing is recommended, T16-T18 are pruned unrun and uncounted; the row reads as no counted support for the transfer, which says little about the real bracket (a different, later biology). Gate passes: counted support that a Feng-bracket direction transfers to T1's platform, consistent with the smoke's known concordance on H and not independent of it; M1's direction stays as ruled. T16 passes: R2 (D, A1) has its first counted support and stands; below N20's similarity floor this is platform evidence only. T16 not passing: R2 stands by his ruling of 2026-11-06 (e); only a mean loss above 0.3 of T16 against lp_proxy on H goes to him as evidence against D and A1, his call. T17 passes against T16, at or above the floor: recommended to him: turn on R2's N1 and N2 lines for M1's real direction (configs/t1_hygiene.yaml lines, T1's s1 and s2 rebuilt through the build's hygiene path and hashed by 24 Nov, T1's s2 back to him under DATA_POLICY 2026-11-04 (d)); below the floor: platform evidence only, no recommendation. T18 passes, at or above the floor: recommended to him: the same rule (feng_dirs.eb_shrink with its deciles, in a new build option, default off) on M1's real direction, the same rebuild route; below the floor: platform evidence only, no recommendation. Each lands only by his ruling. If several pass, each is a separate recommendation, and their combination is not run. Not run, by design: per-type or per-class step fractions (the within-type reads already read the direction per group; a fraction per class would be a free parameter, rule 7), and no candidate tuned on these reads | Fixed now, beyond the rule's controls: a fit, and no transfer claimed, if the margin over b0_scope on a passing read sits mainly on mmd_u or the variogram (the de_recovery and direction groups together below half the total margin, group_margins in the decision file; a coded condition of the pass), or if a pass rests on the E10.5 within-type reads alone (the rule requires H; E10.5's earlier reads, the readout's and diag_v1's, are a named known feature of those reads). A pass on H is consistent with the known concordance of the readout's smoke on this transition (Feng's real direction within CM, on the scorer's metric family) and is no independent evidence that a late Feng bracket transfers. A fail is no evidence against M1's real direction | Amended 2026-11-10 before any run of T13-T18, after N20's first run found that Feng's file has no stage labelled E18.5: the bracket is E14.5 -> E18, the file's own label for its last embryonic stage (read as day 18.0), so 3.5 days and 2/7 of the bracket for a one-day step, not 4 days and 1/4 (`docs/PLAN.md` decision log 2026-11-10 (g)). The commit that adds these rows; code at 73e7b12 (wave2/late-proxy-fix), merged at cdc317f | Run at fc6ebf5 on a clean tree after N20b and a passed preflight (stage gate, `experiments/t1_m1/results/late_proxy_v1/`). GATE FAILED, on its veto alone: `lp_proxy` against `b0_scope` lost 0.70 (0 of 10) on T1/E9.5->E10.5:endocardium, past the 0.3 allowed. The other conditions held: read H +0.91 (8 of 10); T1/E9.5->E10.5:cm +4.14 (10 of 10); both beat Cflip and Cperm by the test and sit on the rank metrics; neither control passes on H. Reported, deciding nothing: T1/E8.5->E9.5 -0.21 (0 of 10), :chamber +0.63 (10 of 10), T1/E9.5->E10.5 +0.35 (10 of 10). | T20 | T1 | `g_mom_flip` (harness candidate, try T20, a sign control of T11; `vec.harness._g_mom_flip`, `vec.t1_state_transport.flip_factors`): g_mom's own draws, B0 base, the fold's fit and stream [seed, 5] (`_g_mom_arm`, the same calls as `_g_mom`, pinned byte-identical under an identity transform by tests/test_g_mom_verify.py), with every finished per-metacell within-type log factor's sign reversed (log f becomes -log f for every metacell and gene; zeros stay zero and the clip is symmetric); R1's held types and the pre-registered held-flat genes as in g_mom | copy_last; candidate g_mom (T11) is read against it, b0 and the floor reported; no entry changes | distribution (mmd_u, 0.30): -0.08 / 15.08; covariance (variogram, 0.20): -1.45 / 11.45 (the groups on which T11 gained) | T11's +1.13 and +1.23 must survive controls before any ruling (decision log 2026-11-10 (e)). Doubt: O1 (T8-T10) found an oracle within-type transport adds only +0.01 on top of an oracle per-type shift on E8.5 -> E9.5, and on both E10.5 cuts B0's base is the cardiomyocyte-heavy E9.5 mix, so the gain need not be momentum. What a sign reversal can say: it keeps every factor's size exactly and inverts only its direction, so it asks whether the gain is odd in the sign of the factor. A finished factor is the metacell's own deviation minus its transported partners', so it is partly a self-amplifier: any state-aligned effect is sign-odd at first order (g_mom expands the state contrast, the flip contracts it by the same amount), so g_mom minus the flip is about twice g_mom minus b0 whether the effect is momentum, amplification of the reference's existing states or a repair of the mix along them. A pass therefore says only that the effect is odd in the factor sign: T20 is nearly implied by T11's gain and is neither a spread control nor evidence of momentum (T23 and T24 are the controls for those) | Facet: none to any entry; a control. Metrics: the paired gain of g_mom over g_mom_flip in board points on both E10.5 cuts, the margins by scored group reported. Expected about twice T11's gains (+2 to +2.5 points) if the effect is sign-odd, near 0 only if it is even in the sign. P(the rule beats the flip on both cuts) about 85% by judgement, no run yet. Spread reading, fixed now and deciding nothing (the metric panel per arm and cut: variance_ratio, mmd_u, variogram, energy_distance, pb_rel_err, in placebo.json): if the effect is sign-odd in the variance, the flip's mean variance_ratio falls near b0's minus what g_mom added, 0.6893 on the whole cut (0.7019 - 0.0126) and 0.7187 on the chamber cut (0.7319 - 0.0132, T11's committed panel), within 0.003 (about 6 seed sds of T11's variance_ratio difference, 0.0004-0.0005). 0 points on any entry; no upload changes | Both counted E10.5 cuts (T1/E9.5->E10.5 and T1/E9.5->E10.5:chamber, configs/pseudo_tasks.yaml; protocol bake, build main, seeds 0-9), one `experiments/harness/run.py run` of floor,b0,g_mom,g_mom_flip,g_mom_perm,g_mom_null,g_mom_held (the same build seeds as T11, so the same draws and B0 base), judged by `experiments/t1_scope/g_mom_verify.py placebo`. Order: first the reproduction gate: this run's b0 within 0.02 points and its g_mom within 0.05 points of try T11's committed per-seed scores on both cuts (experiments/harness/results/t11_g_mom/tasks/; g_mom carries the fit's PCA and k-means floats, b0 does not), else the reading is void and goes to the captain. Rule (one rule with T21, T23 and T24): the momentum reading survives only if g_mom beats EACH control (T20, T21, T23) by a paired mean gain above 0.3 points and in at least 8 of 10 seeds on both cuts (`vec.harness.decide` with the control as base and g_mom as change: the adoption rule's thresholds, no cut losing more than 0.3) and the pseudobulk-held arm T24 keeps its gain over b0 by the same thresholds with its validity gate holding. If it does not, g_mom is not proposed as a within-type momentum and the numbers go to the captain. Every arm is also reported against b0 and against the floor (skill 0.5). Counted under rule 4 as one T1 try | Limits: one transition (E8.5 -> E9.5) and one fit; both cuts share the reference, the start and the draws, so the ten seeds measure draw and scoring noise (T11's paired sd 0.06-0.08) and 'ahead in 8 of 10' is close to automatic once the mean gap exceeds about 0.15: this is one comparison of one fit, not replication, and the two cuts are not independent evidence for the biology (n = 1). Also: a win over the flip only because the flip falls far below b0 while g_mom sits level with b0 (read by comparing both against b0); a gain carried by de_score or de_direction rather than mmd_u and the variogram (reported per group); a pass on one cut only (it fails the rule); and a pass says the effect is sign-odd, nothing more | rows registered at c62eede (T25's wording b748c7a), the captain's go at d4580bf, before the run; run at d4580bf (tree clean; 2026-11-10 06:45 to 07:45 UTC on the DGX) | Ran at d4580bf. BEATEN on both cuts. Reproduction gate of T11 (b0 within 0.02, g_mom within 0.05 per seed): yes, largest difference 0.0000. g_mom minus g_mom_flip: whole +1.68 (10 of 10), chamber +1.85 (10 of 10). Reading fixed in the row: a pass says only that the effect is odd in the factor sign; the variance-based sign-odd reading did not hold on either cut (flip variance_ratio 0.7039 against 0.6893 if sign-odd; chamber 0.7339 against 0.7187). One E8.5 -> E9.5 fit: the seeds are draw and scoring noise, n = 1 for the biology. `experiments/t1_scope/results/t20_t24_controls/`. |
| T21 | T1 | `g_mom_perm` (harness candidate, try T21, an assignment control of T11; `vec.harness._g_mom_perm`, `vec.t1_state_transport.permute_factors`): g_mom's own draws, B0 base, fit and stream [seed, 5] (`_g_mom_arm`), with each category's finished metacell factor rows re-assigned among that category's metacells by one fixed derangement (no metacell keeps its own vector; one generator default_rng(20261110), `vec.harness.G_MOM_PERM_SEED`, visits the categories in sorted order; the same permutation for every build seed and on both cuts, since the fit is the same); R1's held types and held-flat genes as in g_mom | copy_last; candidate g_mom (T11) is read against it, b0 and the floor reported; no entry changes | distribution (mmd_u, 0.30): -0.08 / 15.08; covariance (variogram, 0.20): -1.45 / 11.45 (the groups on which T11 gained) | Doubts (1) and (2): does the gain need each factor on its own state? Which permutation: the whole metacell factor rows (the vectors) re-assigned within their own category, so each vector stays intact and inside its category and only which state receives which vector changes; a derangement because with K as low as 5 a plain permutation leaves about a fifth of the assignment in place; a gene-wise permutation was rejected because it destroys the programmes and the gene-gene structure the variogram reads and so is too easy to beat. What it can say: re-assigning the vectors removes the alignment between a state and its own deviation that a self-amplifier needs, so it tests whether the assignment matters; its 'size and spread are kept' holds in factor space only, not for the population's realised spread (the permuted arm adds spread at second order, without the first-order term g_mom carries). A pass says the assignment matters; it is neither a spread control nor evidence of momentum unless its variance gate holds (T23 and T24 carry those readings) | Facet: none to any entry; a control. Metrics: the paired gain of g_mom over g_mom_perm in board points on both E10.5 cuts, the margins by scored group reported. Expected at least +0.5 if the assignment to states carries the effect (momentum or amplification alike), near 0 only if the gain is the vectors' size. This reports, before any ruling, what the factors would be at test, how far their direction resembles the backtest's against what a split of the same data can reach and what a fit with no development produces, and how much of them is the amplification of each state's own deviation or sits on the technical modules R2 holds flat in Feng's direction | None to any entry. Per fitted category of the test pair: the finished factors' size on the free genes (not the held-flat list): the cell-weighted mean over metacells and genes of abs(log f), the mean and 90% quantile over the entries not zeroed, the share of entries zeroed and at the clip, the number of metacells; the share of the category's E10.5 cells edited and held by R1, the held labels, and the share of free genes with any non-zero factor in any metacell; each edited cell's expected depth after application against its own, per category and over all edited cells (the largest absolute deviation of the ratio from 1, computed with the keep-depth constant `vec.core.apply_factors` returns for these factors; allowed 1e-9, the run stops otherwise), and the realised depth ratio (median, 1%, 99%) after one application on stream [20261110, 5]; the amplification of each fit (test pair, and backtest pair on the categories in both): the regression slope and R^2 through the origin of the finished factors on the metacell's own deviation (metacells cell-weighted, genes equal weight; the deviation is the log of the metacell's own mean CP10k, pseudocount 0.5, minus its cell-weighted mean over the category's metacells); the share of the factor size (l2 and l1) carried by R2's union genes among the free genes (`experiments/t1_hygiene/hygiene.py` `load_union`; not available if the union file is refused), and the number of union genes in the fit; for the categories in both fits, the concordance of the per-category factor direction with the backtest's fit (the absolute cosine of the leading right singular vectors of the two per-metacell factor matrices, each metacell weighted by the square root of its size, free common genes matched by name, and the Spearman correlation of the per-gene cell-weighted factor standard deviations over the genes with a non-zero size in either fit), the same two statistics after projecting the amplification component out of each fit, split-half ceilings (half-data estimates, each fit on half of each stage's cells, so they under-estimate the full-data ceiling: one random halving within every category; default_rng([20261110, 0]) for the test pair and [20261110, 1] for the backtest pair, start stage first; the geometric mean of the two pairs' axis ceilings as the ceiling for the pair-to-pair concordance), a no-development reference (the same two statistics between fits of two random halves of one stage, E10.5's with default_rng([20261110, 2]) and E9.5's with [20261110, 3]: what these statistics read with nothing developing), and the no-development fit's size against g_mom's fit per pair (the ratio of cell-weighted root mean squares over the free genes: the size T23's control is scaled up from). At the test pair g_mom would edit 0.69 of the fitted categories' cells (R1 holds Blood, Early VIC and Macrophage), each edited cell keeping its expected depth (largest deviation 7e-14), with mean |log f| 0.009 on cardiomyocytes and 0.012 on endocardium and no entry at the clip. Against the backtest's fit, the leading axis has |cos| 0.20 (cardiomyocytes) and 0.29 (endocardium), above the no-development references of 0.03 and 0.01 but far below the split-half ceilings of about 0.91 and 0.84; projecting each metacell's own deviation out leaves 0.04 and 0.08. The per-gene size Spearman is 0.79 and 0.68, against no-development references of 0.36 and 0.27 and half-data ceilings of 0.69-0.81 and 0.33-0.82. Amplification of each state's own deviation explains R^2 0.35 and 0.64 of the test pair's factors (0.37 and 0.53 on the backtest). R2's union genes carry 1-3% of the factor size. The no-development fit is about half g_mom's size on both types (0.51 and 0.48 at the test pair, 0.45 and 0.48 on the backtest), the factor T23 scales up.
